# API references to language and VM specifications

[Dependency inventory](../dependencies.md)

This register preserves links outside the release-25 API tree found in the 5,332 current Oracle API HTML
documents. It includes ordinary prose links in addition to declared External Specifications records. Exact
target URLs and source anchors are retained; duplicate target/source/anchor/label combinations are combined.

Edge type: API source reference. A link does not, by itself, incorporate the whole target as a normative
requirement. Read the referenced source passage to determine its force and extent. The source anchor is the
nearest identified source location; a declaration may contain several links and labels. Existing source
records control local-text and edition status.

Records: 509 distinct targets; 1611 source/anchor/label relations.

Six recurring footer link pairs are recorded once in the dependency inventory instead of being repeated for
every API document. No other external link category is excluded.
